codex
The timed kernel is a scalar, unblocked left-looking factorization. I’m going to replace it with a cache-blocked right-looking factorization: each diagonal block is factored once, the block column is solved in parallel, and the trailing lower triangle is updated in parallel with SIMD-friendly loops and synchronization only between those phases. This exposes far more useful work per OpenMP barrier than parallelizing the original inner loops.

+
+// Solve A(rowBegin:rowEnd, kBegin:kEnd) against the factored diagonal tile.
+static void solveBlockColumn(double* const A, const size_t n,
+                             const size_t rowBegin, const size_t rowEnd,
+                             const size_t kBegin, const size_t kEnd) {
+    for (size_t i = rowBegin; i < rowEnd; ++i) {
+        double* const rowI = A + i * n;
+        for (size_t j = kBegin; j < kEnd; ++j) {
+            const double* const rowJ = A + j * n;
+            double sum = 0.0;
+#pragma omp simd reduction(+ : sum)
+            for (size_t p = kBegin; p < j; ++p) {
+                sum += rowI[p] * rowJ[p];
+            }
+            rowI[j] = (rowI[j] - sum) / rowJ[j];
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+// A(i,j) -= L(i,k) * L(j,k)^T. Only the lower half of a diagonal
+// destination tile is stored; off-diagonal destination tiles are rectangular.
+static void updateTrailingTile(double* const A, const size_t n,
+                               const size_t rowBegin, const size_t rowEnd,
+                               const size_t colBegin, const size_t colEnd,
+                               const size_t kBegin, const size_t kEnd) {
+    const bool diagonalTile = rowBegin == colBegin;
+    for (size_t i = rowBegin; i < rowEnd; ++i) {
+        double* const rowI = A + i * n;
+        const size_t lastColumn = diagonalTile ? std::min(colEnd, i + 1) : colEnd;
+        for (size_t j = colBegin; j < lastColumn; ++j) {
+            const double* const rowJ = A + j * n;
+            double sum = 0.0;
+#pragma omp simd reduction(+ : sum)
+            for (size_t p = kBegin; p < kEnd; ++p) {
+                sum += rowI[p] * rowJ[p];
+            }
+            rowI[j] -= sum;
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+// Cache-blocked, right-looking Cholesky decomposition.  OpenMP task
+// dependencies form the tiled Cholesky DAG, allowing work from later block
+// columns to start as soon as its inputs are ready instead of imposing a
+// global barrier after every phase.
+
+bool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
+    if (n == 0) {
+        return true;
+    }
+
+    double* const data = A.data();
+    const size_t tileCount = (n + kTileSize - 1) / kTileSize;
+
+    // One byte per tile is sufficient: dependencies use addresses as tokens,
+    // not the token values.  It also avoids non-contiguous row-major tile
+    // sections in OpenMP depend clauses.
+    std::vector<unsigned char> dependencyTokens(tileCount * tileCount);
+    unsigned char* const tokens = dependencyTokens.data();
+    bool success = true;
+
+#pragma omp parallel default(none) shared(data, n, tileCount, tokens, success)
+    {
+#pragma omp single
+        {
+            for (size_t kTile = 0; kTile < tileCount; ++kTile) {
+                const size_t kBegin = kTile * kTileSize;
+                const size_t kEnd = std::min(n, kBegin + kTileSize);
+                const size_t diagonalToken = kTile * tileCount + kTile;
+
+#pragma omp task firstprivate(kBegin, kEnd, diagonalToken) depend(inout : tokens[diagonalToken]) shared(success) priority(2)
+                {
+                    if (success && !factorDiagonalTile(data, n, kBegin, kEnd)) {
+#pragma omp atomic write
+                        success = false;
+                    }
+                }
+
+                for (size_t iTile = kTile + 1; iTile < tileCount; ++iTile) {
+                    const size_t rowBegin = iTile * kTileSize;
+                    const size_t rowEnd = std::min(n, rowBegin + kTileSize);
+                    const size_t columnToken = iTile * tileCount + kTile;
+
+#pragma omp task firstprivate(rowBegin, rowEnd, kBegin, kEnd, diagonalToken, columnToken) depend(in : tokens[diagonalToken]) depend(inout : tokens[columnToken]) shared(success) priority(1)
+                    {
+                        if (success) {
+                            solveBlockColumn(data, n, rowBegin, rowEnd, kBegin, kEnd);
+                        }
+                    }
+                }
+
+                for (size_t iTile = kTile + 1; iTile < tileCount; ++iTile) {
+                    const size_t rowBegin = iTile * kTileSize;
+                    const size_t rowEnd = std::min(n, rowBegin + kTileSize);
+                    const size_t rowPanelToken = iTile * tileCount + kTile;
+
+                    for (size_t jTile = kTile + 1; jTile <= iTile; ++jTile) {
+                        const size_t colBegin = jTile * kTileSize;
+                        const size_t colEnd = std::min(n, colBegin + kTileSize);
+                        const size_t colPanelToken = jTile * tileCount + kTile;
+                        const size_t outputToken = iTile * tileCount + jTile;
+
+                        if (iTile == jTile) {
+#pragma omp task firstprivate(rowBegin, rowEnd, colBegin, colEnd, kBegin, kEnd, rowPanelToken, outputToken) depend(in : tokens[rowPanelToken]) depend(inout : tokens[outputToken]) shared(success) priority(2)
+                            {
+                                if (success) {
+                                    updateTrailingTile(data, n, rowBegin, rowEnd,
+                                                       colBegin, colEnd, kBegin, kEnd);
+                                }
+                            }
+                        } else {
+#pragma omp task firstprivate(rowBegin, rowEnd, colBegin, colEnd, kBegin, kEnd, rowPanelToken, colPanelToken, outputToken) depend(in : tokens[rowPanelToken], tokens[colPanelToken]) depend(inout : tokens[outputToken]) shared(success)
+                            {
+                                if (success) {
+                                    updateTrailingTile(data, n, rowBegin, rowEnd,
+                                                       colBegin, colEnd, kBegin, kEnd);
+                                }
+                            }
+                        }
+                    }
                 }
-                A[i * n + j] = (A[i * n + j] - sum) / A[j * n + j];
             }
-        }
-        
-        // Zero out upper triangular part
-        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {
-            A[i * n + j] = 0.0;
+#pragma omp taskwait
         }
     }
-    
+
+    if (!success) {
+        return false;
+    }
+
+    // The factorization only consumes the lower triangle. Clear the upper
+    // triangle once, in parallel, after all factorization tasks complete.
+#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
+        std::fill(data + i * n + i + 1, data + (i + 1) * n, 0.0);
+    }
+
     return true;
 }

+                             const size_t rowBegin, const size_t rowEnd,
+                             const size_t kBegin, const size_t kEnd) {
+    for (size_t i = rowBegin; i < rowEnd; ++i) {
+        double* const rowI = A + i * n;
+        for (size_t j = kBegin; j < kEnd; ++j) {
+            const double* const rowJ = A + j * n;
+            double sum = 0.0;
+#pragma omp simd reduction(+ : sum)
+            for (size_t p = kBegin; p < j; ++p) {
+                sum += rowI[p] * rowJ[p];
+            }
+            rowI[j] = (rowI[j] - sum) / rowJ[j];
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+// A(i,j) -= L(i,k) * L(j,k)^T. Only the lower half of a diagonal
+// destination tile is stored; off-diagonal destination tiles are rectangular.
+static void updateTrailingTile(double* const A, const size_t n,
+                               const size_t rowBegin, const size_t rowEnd,
+                               const size_t colBegin, const size_t colEnd,
+                               const size_t kBegin, const size_t kEnd) {
+    const bool diagonalTile = rowBegin == colBegin;
+    for (size_t i = rowBegin; i < rowEnd; ++i) {
+        double* const rowI = A + i * n;
+        const size_t lastColumn = diagonalTile ? std::min(colEnd, i + 1) : colEnd;
+        for (size_t j = colBegin; j < lastColumn; ++j) {
+            const double* const rowJ = A + j * n;
+            double sum = 0.0;
+#pragma omp simd reduction(+ : sum)
+            for (size_t p = kBegin; p < kEnd; ++p) {
+                sum += rowI[p] * rowJ[p];
+            }
+            rowI[j] -= sum;
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+// Cache-blocked, right-looking Cholesky decomposition.  OpenMP task
+// dependencies form the tiled Cholesky DAG, allowing work from later block
+// columns to start as soon as its inputs are ready instead of imposing a
+// global barrier after every phase.
+
+bool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
+    if (n == 0) {
+        return true;
+    }
+
+    double* const data = A.data();
+    const size_t tileCount = (n + kTileSize - 1) / kTileSize;
+
+    // One byte per tile is sufficient: dependencies use addresses as tokens,
+    // not the token values.  It also avoids non-contiguous row-major tile
+    // sections in OpenMP depend clauses.
+    std::vector<unsigned char> dependencyTokens(tileCount * tileCount);
+    unsigned char* const tokens = dependencyTokens.data();
+    bool success = true;
+
+#pragma omp parallel default(none) shared(data, n, tileCount, tokens, success)
+    {
+#pragma omp single
+        {
+            for (size_t kTile = 0; kTile < tileCount; ++kTile) {
+                const size_t kBegin = kTile * kTileSize;
+                const size_t kEnd = std::min(n, kBegin + kTileSize);
+                const size_t diagonalToken = kTile * tileCount + kTile;
+
+#pragma omp task firstprivate(kBegin, kEnd, diagonalToken) depend(inout : tokens[diagonalToken]) shared(success) priority(2)
+                {
+                    if (success && !factorDiagonalTile(data, n, kBegin, kEnd)) {
+#pragma omp atomic write
+                        success = false;
+                    }
+                }
+
+                for (size_t iTile = kTile + 1; iTile < tileCount; ++iTile) {
+                    const size_t rowBegin = iTile * kTileSize;
+                    const size_t rowEnd = std::min(n, rowBegin + kTileSize);
+                    const size_t columnToken = iTile * tileCount + kTile;
+
+#pragma omp task firstprivate(rowBegin, rowEnd, kBegin, kEnd, diagonalToken, columnToken) depend(in : tokens[diagonalToken]) depend(inout : tokens[columnToken]) shared(success) priority(1)
+                    {
+                        if (success) {
+                            solveBlockColumn(data, n, rowBegin, rowEnd, kBegin, kEnd);
+                        }
+                    }
+                }
+
+                for (size_t iTile = kTile + 1; iTile < tileCount; ++iTile) {
+                    const size_t rowBegin = iTile * kTileSize;
+                    const size_t rowEnd = std::min(n, rowBegin + kTileSize);
+                    const size_t rowPanelToken = iTile * tileCount + kTile;
+
+                    for (size_t jTile = kTile + 1; jTile <= iTile; ++jTile) {
+                        const size_t colBegin = jTile * kTileSize;
+                        const size_t colEnd = std::min(n, colBegin + kTileSize);
+                        const size_t colPanelToken = jTile * tileCount + kTile;
+                        const size_t outputToken = iTile * tileCount + jTile;
+
+                        if (iTile == jTile) {
+#pragma omp task firstprivate(rowBegin, rowEnd, colBegin, colEnd, kBegin, kEnd, rowPanelToken, outputToken) depend(in : tokens[rowPanelToken]) depend(inout : tokens[outputToken]) shared(success) priority(2)
+                            {
+                                if (success) {
+                                    updateTrailingTile(data, n, rowBegin, rowEnd,
+                                                       colBegin, colEnd, kBegin, kEnd);
+                                }
+                            }
+                        } else {
+#pragma omp task firstprivate(rowBegin, rowEnd, colBegin, colEnd, kBegin, kEnd, rowPanelToken, colPanelToken, outputToken) depend(in : tokens[rowPanelToken], tokens[colPanelToken]) depend(inout : tokens[outputToken]) shared(success)
+                            {
+                                if (success) {
+                                    updateTrailingTile(data, n, rowBegin, rowEnd,
+                                                       colBegin, colEnd, kBegin, kEnd);
+                                }
+                            }
+                        }
+                    }
                 }
-                A[i * n + j] = (A[i * n + j] - sum) / A[j * n + j];
             }
-        }
-        
-        // Zero out upper triangular part
-        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {
-            A[i * n + j] = 0.0;
+#pragma omp taskwait
         }
     }
-    
+
+    if (!success) {
+        return false;
+    }
+
+    // The factorization only consumes the lower triangle. Clear the upper
+    // triangle once, in parallel, after all factorization tasks complete.
+#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
+        std::fill(data + i * n + i + 1, data + (i + 1) * n, 0.0);
+    }
+
     return true;
 }

+// Solve A(rowBegin:rowEnd, kBegin:kEnd) against the factored diagonal tile.
+static void solveBlockColumn(double* const A, const size_t n,
+                             const size_t rowBegin, const size_t rowEnd,
+                             const size_t kBegin, const size_t kEnd) {
+    for (size_t i = rowBegin; i < rowEnd; ++i) {
+        double* const rowI = A + i * n;
+        for (size_t j = kBegin; j < kEnd; ++j) {
+            const double* const rowJ = A + j * n;
+            double sum = 0.0;
+#pragma omp simd reduction(+ : sum)
+            for (size_t p = kBegin; p < j; ++p) {
+                sum += rowI[p] * rowJ[p];

+// A(i,j) -= L(i,k) * L(j,k)^T. Only the lower half of a diagonal
+// destination tile is stored; off-diagonal destination tiles are rectangular.
+static void updateTrailingTile(double* const A, const size_t n,
+                               const size_t rowBegin, const size_t rowEnd,
+                               const size_t colBegin, const size_t colEnd,
+                               const size_t kBegin, const size_t kEnd) {
+    const bool diagonalTile = rowBegin == colBegin;
+    for (size_t i = rowBegin; i < rowEnd; ++i) {
+        double* const rowI = A + i * n;
+        const size_t lastColumn = diagonalTile ? std::min(colEnd, i + 1) : colEnd;
+        for (size_t j = colBegin; j < lastColumn; ++j) {
+            const double* const rowJ = A + j * n;
+            double sum = 0.0;
+#pragma omp simd reduction(+ : sum)

+            }
+            rowI[j] -= sum;

+// Cache-blocked, right-looking Cholesky decomposition.  OpenMP task
+// dependencies form the tiled Cholesky DAG, allowing work from later block
+// columns to start as soon as its inputs are ready instead of imposing a
+// global barrier after every phase.
+
+bool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
+    if (n == 0) {
+        return true;
+    }
+
+    double* const data = A.data();
+    const size_t tileCount = (n + kTileSize - 1) / kTileSize;
+
+    // One byte per tile is sufficient: dependencies use addresses as tokens,
+    // not the token values.  It also avoids non-contiguous row-major tile
+    // sections in OpenMP depend clauses.
+    std::vector<unsigned char> dependencyTokens(tileCount * tileCount);
+    unsigned char* const tokens = dependencyTokens.data();
+    std::atomic_bool success{true};
+
+#pragma omp parallel default(none) shared(data, n, tileCount, tokens, success)
+    {
+#pragma omp single
+        {
+            for (size_t kTile = 0; kTile < tileCount; ++kTile) {
+                const size_t kBegin = kTile * kTileSize;
+                const size_t kEnd = std::min(n, kBegin + kTileSize);
+                const size_t diagonalToken = kTile * tileCount + kTile;
+
+#pragma omp task firstprivate(kBegin, kEnd, diagonalToken) depend(inout : tokens[diagonalToken]) shared(success) priority(2)
+                {
+                    if (success.load(std::memory_order_relaxed) &&
+                        !factorDiagonalTile(data, n, kBegin, kEnd)) {
+                        success.store(false, std::memory_order_relaxed);
+                    }
+                }
+
+                for (size_t iTile = kTile + 1; iTile < tileCount; ++iTile) {
+                    const size_t rowBegin = iTile * kTileSize;
+                    const size_t rowEnd = std::min(n, rowBegin + kTileSize);
+                    const size_t columnToken = iTile * tileCount + kTile;
+
+#pragma omp task firstprivate(rowBegin, rowEnd, kBegin, kEnd, diagonalToken, columnToken) depend(in : tokens[diagonalToken]) depend(inout : tokens[columnToken]) shared(success) priority(1)
+                    {
+                        if (success.load(std::memory_order_relaxed)) {
+                            solveBlockColumn(data, n, rowBegin, rowEnd, kBegin, kEnd);
+                        }
+                    }
+                }
+
+                for (size_t iTile = kTile + 1; iTile < tileCount; ++iTile) {
+                    const size_t rowBegin = iTile * kTileSize;
+                    const size_t rowEnd = std::min(n, rowBegin + kTileSize);
+                    const size_t rowPanelToken = iTile * tileCount + kTile;
+
+                    for (size_t jTile = kTile + 1; jTile <= iTile; ++jTile) {
+                        const size_t colBegin = jTile * kTileSize;
+                        const size_t colEnd = std::min(n, colBegin + kTileSize);
+                        const size_t colPanelToken = jTile * tileCount + kTile;
+                        const size_t outputToken = iTile * tileCount + jTile;
+
+                        if (iTile == jTile) {
+#pragma omp task firstprivate(rowBegin, rowEnd, colBegin, colEnd, kBegin, kEnd, rowPanelToken, outputToken) depend(in : tokens[rowPanelToken]) depend(inout : tokens[outputToken]) shared(success) priority(2)
+                            {
+                                if (success.load(std::memory_order_relaxed)) {
+                                    updateTrailingTile(data, n, rowBegin, rowEnd,
+                                                       colBegin, colEnd, kBegin, kEnd);
+                                }
+                            }
+                        } else {
+#pragma omp task firstprivate(rowBegin, rowEnd, colBegin, colEnd, kBegin, kEnd, rowPanelToken, colPanelToken, outputToken) depend(in : tokens[rowPanelToken], tokens[colPanelToken]) depend(inout : tokens[outputToken]) shared(success)
+                            {
+                                if (success.load(std::memory_order_relaxed)) {
+                                    updateTrailingTile(data, n, rowBegin, rowEnd,
+                                                       colBegin, colEnd, kBegin, kEnd);
+                                }
+                            }
+                        }
+                    }
+                }
+            }
+#pragma omp taskwait
+        }
+    }
+
+    if (!success.load(std::memory_order_relaxed)) {
+        return false;
+    }
+
+    // The factorization only consumes the lower triangle. Clear the upper
+    // triangle once, in parallel, after all factorization tasks complete.
+#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
+        std::fill(data + i * n + i + 1, data + (i + 1) * n, 0.0);
+    }
+
     return true;
 }

+// A(i,j) -= L(i,k) * L(j,k)^T. Only the lower half of a diagonal

+// Cache-blocked, right-looking Cholesky decomposition.  OpenMP task
+// dependencies form the tiled Cholesky DAG, allowing work from later block
+// columns to start as soon as its inputs are ready instead of imposing a
+// global barrier after every phase.
+
+bool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
+    if (n == 0) {
+        return true;
+    }
+
+    double* const data = A.data();
+    const size_t tileCount = (n + kTileSize - 1) / kTileSize;
+
+    // One byte per tile is sufficient: dependencies use addresses as tokens,
+    // not the token values.  It also avoids non-contiguous row-major tile
+    // sections in OpenMP depend clauses.
+    std::vector<unsigned char> dependencyTokens(tileCount * tileCount);
+    unsigned char* const tokens = dependencyTokens.data();
+    std::atomic_bool success{true};
+
+#pragma omp parallel default(none) shared(data, n, tileCount, tokens, success)
+    {
+#pragma omp single
+        {
+            for (size_t kTile = 0; kTile < tileCount; ++kTile) {
+                const size_t kBegin = kTile * kTileSize;
+                const size_t kEnd = std::min(n, kBegin + kTileSize);
+                const size_t diagonalToken = kTile * tileCount + kTile;
+
+#pragma omp task firstprivate(kBegin, kEnd, diagonalToken) depend(inout : tokens[diagonalToken]) shared(success) priority(2)
+                {
+                    if (success.load(std::memory_order_relaxed) &&
+                        !factorDiagonalTile(data, n, kBegin, kEnd)) {
+                        success.store(false, std::memory_order_relaxed);
+                    }
+                }
+
+                for (size_t iTile = kTile + 1; iTile < tileCount; ++iTile) {
+                    const size_t rowBegin = iTile * kTileSize;
+                    const size_t rowEnd = std::min(n, rowBegin + kTileSize);
+                    const size_t columnToken = iTile * tileCount + kTile;
+
+#pragma omp task firstprivate(rowBegin, rowEnd, kBegin, kEnd, diagonalToken, columnToken) depend(in : tokens[diagonalToken]) depend(inout : tokens[columnToken]) shared(success) priority(1)
+                    {
+                        if (success.load(std::memory_order_relaxed)) {
+                            solveBlockColumn(data, n, rowBegin, rowEnd, kBegin, kEnd);
+                        }
+                    }
+                }
+
+                for (size_t iTile = kTile + 1; iTile < tileCount; ++iTile) {
+                    const size_t rowBegin = iTile * kTileSize;
+                    const size_t rowEnd = std::min(n, rowBegin + kTileSize);
+                    const size_t rowPanelToken = iTile * tileCount + kTile;
+
+                    for (size_t jTile = kTile + 1; jTile <= iTile; ++jTile) {
+                        const size_t colBegin = jTile * kTileSize;
+                        const size_t colEnd = std::min(n, colBegin + kTileSize);
+                        const size_t colPanelToken = jTile * tileCount + kTile;
+                        const size_t outputToken = iTile * tileCount + jTile;
+
+                        if (iTile == jTile) {
+#pragma omp task firstprivate(rowBegin, rowEnd, colBegin, colEnd, kBegin, kEnd, rowPanelToken, outputToken) depend(in : tokens[rowPanelToken]) depend(inout : tokens[outputToken]) shared(success) priority(2)
+                            {
+                                if (success.load(std::memory_order_relaxed)) {
+                                    updateTrailingTile(data, n, rowBegin, rowEnd,
+                                                       colBegin, colEnd, kBegin, kEnd);
+                                }
+                            }
+                        } else {
+#pragma omp task firstprivate(rowBegin, rowEnd, colBegin, colEnd, kBegin, kEnd, rowPanelToken, colPanelToken, outputToken) depend(in : tokens[rowPanelToken], tokens[colPanelToken]) depend(inout : tokens[outputToken]) shared(success)
+                            {
+                                if (success.load(std::memory_order_relaxed)) {
+                                    updateTrailingTile(data, n, rowBegin, rowEnd,
+                                                       colBegin, colEnd, kBegin, kEnd);
+                                }
+                            }
+                        }
+                    }
+                }
+            }
+#pragma omp taskwait
+        }
+    }
+
+    if (!success.load(std::memory_order_relaxed)) {
+        return false;
+    }
+
+    // The factorization only consumes the lower triangle. Clear the upper
+    // triangle once, in parallel, after all factorization tasks complete.
+#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {

+                             const size_t kBegin, const size_t kEnd) {

+// A(i,j) -= L(i,k) * L(j,k)^T. Only the lower half of a diagonal
+// destination tile is stored; off-diagonal destination tiles are rectangular.
+static void updateTrailingTile(double* const A, const size_t n,
+                               const size_t rowBegin, const size_t rowEnd,
+                               const size_t colBegin, const size_t colEnd,
+                               const size_t kBegin, const size_t kEnd) {
+    const bool diagonalTile = rowBegin == colBegin;
+    for (size_t i = rowBegin; i < rowEnd; ++i) {
+        double* const rowI = A + i * n;
+        const size_t lastColumn = diagonalTile ? std::min(colEnd, i + 1) : colEnd;
+        for (size_t j = colBegin; j < lastColumn; ++j) {

+            double sum = 0.0;
+#pragma omp simd reduction(+ : sum)
+            for (size_t p = kBegin; p < kEnd; ++p) {
+                sum += rowI[p] * rowJ[p];
+            }
+            rowI[j] -= sum;
+        }

+// Cache-blocked, right-looking Cholesky decomposition.  OpenMP task
+// dependencies form the tiled Cholesky DAG, allowing work from later block
+// columns to start as soon as its inputs are ready instead of imposing a
+// global barrier after every phase.
+
+bool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
+    if (n == 0) {
+        return true;
+    }
+
+    double* const data = A.data();
+    const size_t tileCount = (n + kTileSize - 1) / kTileSize;
+
+    // One byte per tile is sufficient: dependencies use addresses as tokens,
+    // not the token values.  It also avoids non-contiguous row-major tile
+    // sections in OpenMP depend clauses.
+    std::vector<unsigned char> dependencyTokens(tileCount * tileCount);
+    unsigned char* const tokens = dependencyTokens.data();
+    std::atomic_bool success{true};
+
+#pragma omp parallel default(none) shared(data, n, tileCount, tokens, success)
+    {
+#pragma omp single
+        {
+            for (size_t kTile = 0; kTile < tileCount; ++kTile) {
+                const size_t kBegin = kTile * kTileSize;
+                const size_t kEnd = std::min(n, kBegin + kTileSize);
+                const size_t diagonalToken = kTile * tileCount + kTile;
+
+#pragma omp task firstprivate(kBegin, kEnd, diagonalToken) depend(inout : tokens[diagonalToken]) shared(success) priority(2)
+                {
+                    if (success.load(std::memory_order_relaxed) &&
+                        !factorDiagonalTile(data, n, kBegin, kEnd)) {
+                        success.store(false, std::memory_order_relaxed);
+                    }
+                }
+
+                for (size_t iTile = kTile + 1; iTile < tileCount; ++iTile) {
+                    const size_t rowBegin = iTile * kTileSize;
+                    const size_t rowEnd = std::min(n, rowBegin + kTileSize);
+                    const size_t columnToken = iTile * tileCount + kTile;
+
+#pragma omp task firstprivate(rowBegin, rowEnd, kBegin, kEnd, diagonalToken, columnToken) depend(in : tokens[diagonalToken]) depend(inout : tokens[columnToken]) shared(success) priority(1)
+                    {
+                        if (success.load(std::memory_order_relaxed)) {
+                            solveBlockColumn(data, n, rowBegin, rowEnd, kBegin, kEnd);
+                        }
+                    }
+                }
+
+                for (size_t iTile = kTile + 1; iTile < tileCount; ++iTile) {
+                    const size_t rowBegin = iTile * kTileSize;
+                    const size_t rowEnd = std::min(n, rowBegin + kTileSize);
+                    const size_t rowPanelToken = iTile * tileCount + kTile;
+
+                    for (size_t jTile = kTile + 1; jTile <= iTile; ++jTile) {
+                        const size_t colBegin = jTile * kTileSize;
+                        const size_t colEnd = std::min(n, colBegin + kTileSize);
+                        const size_t colPanelToken = jTile * tileCount + kTile;
+                        const size_t outputToken = iTile * tileCount + jTile;
+
+                        if (iTile == jTile) {
+#pragma omp task firstprivate(rowBegin, rowEnd, colBegin, colEnd, kBegin, kEnd, rowPanelToken, outputToken) depend(in : tokens[rowPanelToken]) depend(inout : tokens[outputToken]) shared(success) priority(2)
+                            {
+                                if (success.load(std::memory_order_relaxed)) {
+                                    updateTrailingTile(data, n, rowBegin, rowEnd,
+                                                       colBegin, colEnd, kBegin, kEnd);
+                                }
+                            }
+                        } else {
+#pragma omp task firstprivate(rowBegin, rowEnd, colBegin, colEnd, kBegin, kEnd, rowPanelToken, colPanelToken, outputToken) depend(in : tokens[rowPanelToken], tokens[colPanelToken]) depend(inout : tokens[outputToken]) shared(success)
+                            {
+                                if (success.load(std::memory_order_relaxed)) {
+                                    updateTrailingTile(data, n, rowBegin, rowEnd,
+                                                       colBegin, colEnd, kBegin, kEnd);
+                                }
+                            }
+                        }
+                    }
+                }
+            }
+#pragma omp taskwait
+        }
+    }
+
+    if (!success.load(std::memory_order_relaxed)) {
+        return false;
+    }
+
+    // The factorization only consumes the lower triangle. Clear the upper
+    // triangle once, in parallel, after all factorization tasks complete.
+#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
+        std::fill(data + i * n + i + 1, data + (i + 1) * n, 0.0);
+    }
+
     return true;
 }

+// Solve A(rowBegin:rowEnd, kBegin:kEnd) against the factored diagonal tile.

+                             const size_t kBegin, const size_t kEnd) {

+        double* const rowI = A + i * n;
+        for (size_t j = kBegin; j < kEnd; ++j) {

+// A(i,j) -= L(i,k) * L(j,k)^T. Only the lower half of a diagonal
+// destination tile is stored; off-diagonal destination tiles are rectangular.
+static void updateTrailingTile(double* const A, const size_t n,
+                               const size_t rowBegin, const size_t rowEnd,
+                               const size_t colBegin, const size_t colEnd,
+                               const size_t kBegin, const size_t kEnd) {
+    const bool diagonalTile = rowBegin == colBegin;
+    for (size_t i = rowBegin; i < rowEnd; ++i) {
+        double* const rowI = A + i * n;
+        const size_t lastColumn = diagonalTile ? std::min(colEnd, i + 1) : colEnd;
+        for (size_t j = colBegin; j < lastColumn; ++j) {
+            const double* const rowJ = A + j * n;
+            double sum = 0.0;
+#pragma omp simd reduction(+ : sum)
+            for (size_t p = kBegin; p < kEnd; ++p) {
+                sum += rowI[p] * rowJ[p];
+            }
+            rowI[j] -= sum;
+        }
+    }

+
+// Cache-blocked, right-looking Cholesky decomposition.  OpenMP task
+// dependencies form the tiled Cholesky DAG, allowing work from later block
+// columns to start as soon as its inputs are ready instead of imposing a
+// global barrier after every phase.
+
+bool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
+    if (n == 0) {
+        return true;
+    }
+
+    double* const data = A.data();
+    const size_t tileCount = (n + kTileSize - 1) / kTileSize;
+
+    // One byte per tile is sufficient: dependencies use addresses as tokens,
+    // not the token values.  It also avoids non-contiguous row-major tile
+    // sections in OpenMP depend clauses.
+    std::vector<unsigned char> dependencyTokens(tileCount * tileCount);
+    unsigned char* const tokens = dependencyTokens.data();
+    std::atomic_bool success{true};
+
+#pragma omp parallel default(none) shared(data, n, tileCount, tokens, success)
+    {
+#pragma omp single
+        {
+            for (size_t kTile = 0; kTile < tileCount; ++kTile) {
+                const size_t kBegin = kTile * kTileSize;
+                const size_t kEnd = std::min(n, kBegin + kTileSize);
+                const size_t diagonalToken = kTile * tileCount + kTile;
+
+#pragma omp task firstprivate(kBegin, kEnd, diagonalToken) depend(inout : tokens[diagonalToken]) shared(success) priority(2)
+                {
+                    if (success.load(std::memory_order_relaxed) &&
+                        !factorDiagonalTile(data, n, kBegin, kEnd)) {
+                        success.store(false, std::memory_order_relaxed);
+                    }
+                }
+
+                for (size_t iTile = kTile + 1; iTile < tileCount; ++iTile) {
+                    const size_t rowBegin = iTile * kTileSize;
+                    const size_t rowEnd = std::min(n, rowBegin + kTileSize);
+                    const size_t columnToken = iTile * tileCount + kTile;
+
+#pragma omp task firstprivate(rowBegin, rowEnd, kBegin, kEnd, diagonalToken, columnToken) depend(in : tokens[diagonalToken]) depend(inout : tokens[columnToken]) shared(success) priority(1)
+                    {
+                        if (success.load(std::memory_order_relaxed)) {
+                            solveBlockColumn(data, n, rowBegin, rowEnd, kBegin, kEnd);
+                        }
+                    }
+                }
+
+                for (size_t iTile = kTile + 1; iTile < tileCount; ++iTile) {
+                    const size_t rowBegin = iTile * kTileSize;
+                    const size_t rowEnd = std::min(n, rowBegin + kTileSize);
+                    const size_t rowPanelToken = iTile * tileCount + kTile;
+
+                    for (size_t jTile = kTile + 1; jTile <= iTile; ++jTile) {
+                        const size_t colBegin = jTile * kTileSize;
+                        const size_t colEnd = std::min(n, colBegin + kTileSize);
+                        const size_t colPanelToken = jTile * tileCount + kTile;
+                        const size_t outputToken = iTile * tileCount + jTile;
+
+                        if (iTile == jTile) {
+#pragma omp task firstprivate(rowBegin, rowEnd, colBegin, colEnd, kBegin, kEnd, rowPanelToken, outputToken) depend(in : tokens[rowPanelToken]) depend(inout : tokens[outputToken]) shared(success) priority(2)
+                            {
+                                if (success.load(std::memory_order_relaxed)) {
+                                    updateTrailingTile(data, n, rowBegin, rowEnd,
+                                                       colBegin, colEnd, kBegin, kEnd);
+                                }
+                            }
+                        } else {
+#pragma omp task firstprivate(rowBegin, rowEnd, colBegin, colEnd, kBegin, kEnd, rowPanelToken, colPanelToken, outputToken) depend(in : tokens[rowPanelToken], tokens[colPanelToken]) depend(inout : tokens[outputToken]) shared(success)
+                            {
+                                if (success.load(std::memory_order_relaxed)) {
+                                    updateTrailingTile(data, n, rowBegin, rowEnd,
+                                                       colBegin, colEnd, kBegin, kEnd);
+                                }
+                            }
+                        }
+                    }
+                }
+            }
+#pragma omp taskwait
+        }
+    }
+
+    if (!success.load(std::memory_order_relaxed)) {
+        return false;
+    }
+
+    // The factorization only consumes the lower triangle. Clear the upper
+    // triangle once, in parallel, after all factorization tasks complete.
+#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
+        std::fill(data + i * n + i + 1, data + (i + 1) * n, 0.0);
+    }
+
     return true;
 }

+// Solve A(rowBegin:rowEnd, kBegin:kEnd) against the factored diagonal tile.
+static void solveBlockColumn(double* const A, const size_t n,

+                             const size_t kBegin, const size_t kEnd) {
+    for (size_t i = rowBegin; i < rowEnd; ++i) {
+        double* const rowI = A + i * n;
+        for (size_t j = kBegin; j < kEnd; ++j) {

+// A(i,j) -= L(i,k) * L(j,k)^T. Only the lower half of a diagonal
+// destination tile is stored; off-diagonal destination tiles are rectangular.
+static void updateTrailingTile(double* const A, const size_t n,
+                               const size_t rowBegin, const size_t rowEnd,
+                               const size_t colBegin, const size_t colEnd,
+                               const size_t kBegin, const size_t kEnd) {
+    const bool diagonalTile = rowBegin == colBegin;
+    for (size_t i = rowBegin; i < rowEnd; ++i) {

+        const size_t lastColumn = diagonalTile ? std::min(colEnd, i + 1) : colEnd;

+// Cache-blocked, right-looking Cholesky decomposition.  OpenMP task
+// dependencies form the tiled Cholesky DAG, allowing work from later block
+// columns to start as soon as its inputs are ready instead of imposing a
+// global barrier after every phase.
+
+bool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
+    if (n == 0) {
+        return true;
+    }
+
+    double* const data = A.data();
+    const size_t tileCount = (n + kTileSize - 1) / kTileSize;
+
+    // One byte per tile is sufficient: dependencies use addresses as tokens,
+    // not the token values.  It also avoids non-contiguous row-major tile
+    // sections in OpenMP depend clauses.
+    std::vector<unsigned char> dependencyTokens(tileCount * tileCount);
+    unsigned char* const tokens = dependencyTokens.data();
+    std::atomic_bool success{true};
+
+#pragma omp parallel default(none) shared(data, n, tileCount, tokens, success)
+    {
+#pragma omp single
+        {
+            for (size_t kTile = 0; kTile < tileCount; ++kTile) {
+                const size_t kBegin = kTile * kTileSize;
+                const size_t kEnd = std::min(n, kBegin + kTileSize);
+                const size_t diagonalToken = kTile * tileCount + kTile;
+
+#pragma omp task firstprivate(kBegin, kEnd, diagonalToken) depend(inout : tokens[diagonalToken]) shared(success) priority(2)
+                {
+                    if (success.load(std::memory_order_relaxed) &&
+                        !factorDiagonalTile(data, n, kBegin, kEnd)) {
+                        success.store(false, std::memory_order_relaxed);
+                    }
+                }
+
+                for (size_t iTile = kTile + 1; iTile < tileCount; ++iTile) {
+                    const size_t rowBegin = iTile * kTileSize;
+                    const size_t rowEnd = std::min(n, rowBegin + kTileSize);
+                    const size_t columnToken = iTile * tileCount + kTile;
+
+#pragma omp task firstprivate(rowBegin, rowEnd, kBegin, kEnd, diagonalToken, columnToken) depend(in : tokens[diagonalToken]) depend(inout : tokens[columnToken]) shared(success) priority(1)
+                    {
+                        if (success.load(std::memory_order_relaxed)) {
+                            solveBlockColumn(data, n, rowBegin, rowEnd, kBegin, kEnd);
+                        }
+                    }
+                }
+
+                for (size_t iTile = kTile + 1; iTile < tileCount; ++iTile) {
+                    const size_t rowBegin = iTile * kTileSize;
+                    const size_t rowEnd = std::min(n, rowBegin + kTileSize);
+                    const size_t rowPanelToken = iTile * tileCount + kTile;
+
+                    for (size_t jTile = kTile + 1; jTile <= iTile; ++jTile) {
+                        const size_t colBegin = jTile * kTileSize;
+                        const size_t colEnd = std::min(n, colBegin + kTileSize);
+                        const size_t colPanelToken = jTile * tileCount + kTile;
+                        const size_t outputToken = iTile * tileCount + jTile;
+
+                        if (iTile == jTile) {
+#pragma omp task firstprivate(rowBegin, rowEnd, colBegin, colEnd, kBegin, kEnd, rowPanelToken, outputToken) depend(in : tokens[rowPanelToken]) depend(inout : tokens[outputToken]) shared(success) priority(2)
+                            {
+                                if (success.load(std::memory_order_relaxed)) {
+                                    updateTrailingTile(data, n, rowBegin, rowEnd,
+                                                       colBegin, colEnd, kBegin, kEnd);
+                                }
+                            }
+                        } else {
+#pragma omp task firstprivate(rowBegin, rowEnd, colBegin, colEnd, kBegin, kEnd, rowPanelToken, colPanelToken, outputToken) depend(in : tokens[rowPanelToken], tokens[colPanelToken]) depend(inout : tokens[outputToken]) shared(success)
+                            {
+                                if (success.load(std::memory_order_relaxed)) {
+                                    updateTrailingTile(data, n, rowBegin, rowEnd,
+                                                       colBegin, colEnd, kBegin, kEnd);
+                                }
+                            }
+                        }
+                    }
+                }
+            }
+#pragma omp taskwait
+        }
+    }
+
+    if (!success.load(std::memory_order_relaxed)) {
+        return false;
+    }
+
+    // The factorization only consumes the lower triangle. Clear the upper
+    // triangle once, in parallel, after all factorization tasks complete.
+#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
+        std::fill(data + i * n + i + 1, data + (i + 1) * n, 0.0);
+    }
+
     return true;
 }

+static void solveBlockColumn(double* const A, const size_t n,

+                             const size_t kBegin, const size_t kEnd) {

+        double* const rowI = A + i * n;

+// A(i,j) -= L(i,k) * L(j,k)^T. Only the lower half of a diagonal
+// destination tile is stored; off-diagonal destination tiles are rectangular.
+static void updateTrailingTile(double* const A, const size_t n,
+                               const size_t rowBegin, const size_t rowEnd,
+                               const size_t colBegin, const size_t colEnd,
+                               const size_t kBegin, const size_t kEnd) {
+    const bool diagonalTile = rowBegin == colBegin;
+    for (size_t i = rowBegin; i < rowEnd; ++i) {
+        double* const rowI = A + i * n;
+        const size_t lastColumn = diagonalTile ? std::min(colEnd, i + 1) : colEnd;

+            for (size_t p = kBegin; p < kEnd; ++p) {
+                sum += rowI[p] * rowJ[p];
+            }
+            rowI[j] -= sum;
+        }
+    }

+// Cache-blocked, right-looking Cholesky decomposition.  OpenMP task
+// dependencies form the tiled Cholesky DAG, allowing work from later block
+// columns to start as soon as its inputs are ready instead of imposing a
+// global barrier after every phase.
+
+bool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
+    if (n == 0) {
+        return true;
+    }
+
+    double* const data = A.data();
+    const size_t tileCount = (n + kTileSize - 1) / kTileSize;
+
+    // One byte per tile is sufficient: dependencies use addresses as tokens,
+    // not the token values.  It also avoids non-contiguous row-major tile
+    // sections in OpenMP depend clauses.
+    std::vector<unsigned char> dependencyTokens(tileCount * tileCount);
+    unsigned char* const tokens = dependencyTokens.data();
+    std::atomic_bool success{true};
+
+#pragma omp parallel default(none) shared(data, n, tileCount, tokens, success)
+    {
+#pragma omp single
+        {
+            for (size_t kTile = 0; kTile < tileCount; ++kTile) {
+                const size_t kBegin = kTile * kTileSize;
+                const size_t kEnd = std::min(n, kBegin + kTileSize);
+                const size_t diagonalToken = kTile * tileCount + kTile;
+
+#pragma omp task firstprivate(kBegin, kEnd, diagonalToken) depend(inout : tokens[diagonalToken]) shared(success) priority(2)
+                {
+                    if (success.load(std::memory_order_relaxed) &&
+                        !factorDiagonalTile(data, n, kBegin, kEnd)) {
+                        success.store(false, std::memory_order_relaxed);
+                    }
+                }
+
+                for (size_t iTile = kTile + 1; iTile < tileCount; ++iTile) {
+                    const size_t rowBegin = iTile * kTileSize;
+                    const size_t rowEnd = std::min(n, rowBegin + kTileSize);
+                    const size_t columnToken = iTile * tileCount + kTile;
+
+#pragma omp task firstprivate(rowBegin, rowEnd, kBegin, kEnd, diagonalToken, columnToken) depend(in : tokens[diagonalToken]) depend(inout : tokens[columnToken]) shared(success) priority(1)
+                    {
+                        if (success.load(std::memory_order_relaxed)) {
+                            solveBlockColumn(data, n, rowBegin, rowEnd, kBegin, kEnd);
+                        }
+                    }
+                }
+
+                for (size_t iTile = kTile + 1; iTile < tileCount; ++iTile) {
+                    const size_t rowBegin = iTile * kTileSize;
+                    const size_t rowEnd = std::min(n, rowBegin + kTileSize);
+                    const size_t rowPanelToken = iTile * tileCount + kTile;
+
+                    for (size_t jTile = kTile + 1; jTile <= iTile; ++jTile) {
+                        const size_t colBegin = jTile * kTileSize;
+                        const size_t colEnd = std::min(n, colBegin + kTileSize);
+                        const size_t colPanelToken = jTile * tileCount + kTile;
+                        const size_t outputToken = iTile * tileCount + jTile;
+
+                        if (iTile == jTile) {
+#pragma omp task firstprivate(rowBegin, rowEnd, colBegin, colEnd, kBegin, kEnd, rowPanelToken, outputToken) depend(in : tokens[rowPanelToken]) depend(inout : tokens[outputToken]) shared(success) priority(2)
+                            {
+                                if (success.load(std::memory_order_relaxed)) {
+                                    updateTrailingTile(data, n, rowBegin, rowEnd,
+                                                       colBegin, colEnd, kBegin, kEnd);
+                                }
+                            }
+                        } else {
+#pragma omp task firstprivate(rowBegin, rowEnd, colBegin, colEnd, kBegin, kEnd, rowPanelToken, colPanelToken, outputToken) depend(in : tokens[rowPanelToken], tokens[colPanelToken]) depend(inout : tokens[outputToken]) shared(success)
+                            {
+                                if (success.load(std::memory_order_relaxed)) {
+                                    updateTrailingTile(data, n, rowBegin, rowEnd,
+                                                       colBegin, colEnd, kBegin, kEnd);
+                                }
+                            }
+                        }
+                    }
+                }
+            }
+#pragma omp taskwait
+        }
+    }
+
+    if (!success.load(std::memory_order_relaxed)) {
+        return false;
+    }
+
+    // The factorization only consumes the lower triangle. Clear the upper
+    // triangle once, in parallel, after all factorization tasks complete.
+#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
+        std::fill(data + i * n + i + 1, data + (i + 1) * n, 0.0);
+    }
+
     return true;

+// Cache-blocked, right-looking Cholesky decomposition.  OpenMP task
+// dependencies form the tiled Cholesky DAG, allowing work from later block
+// columns to start as soon as its inputs are ready instead of imposing a
+// global barrier after every phase.
+
+bool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
+    if (n == 0) {
+        return true;
+    }
+
+    double* const data = A.data();
+    const size_t tileCount = (n + kTileSize - 1) / kTileSize;
+
+    // One byte per tile is sufficient: dependencies use addresses as tokens,
+    // not the token values.  It also avoids non-contiguous row-major tile
+    // sections in OpenMP depend clauses.
+    std::vector<unsigned char> dependencyTokens(tileCount * tileCount);

+    std::atomic_bool success{true};
+

+    {
+#pragma omp single
+        {
+            for (size_t kTile = 0; kTile < tileCount; ++kTile) {
+                const size_t kBegin = kTile * kTileSize;
+                const size_t kEnd = std::min(n, kBegin + kTileSize);
+                const size_t diagonalToken = kTile * tileCount + kTile;
+
+#pragma omp task firstprivate(kBegin, kEnd, diagonalToken) depend(inout : tokens[diagonalToken]) shared(success) priority(2)
+                {
+                    if (success.load(std::memory_order_relaxed) &&
+                        !factorDiagonalTile(data, n, kBegin, kEnd)) {
+                        success.store(false, std::memory_order_relaxed);
+                    }
+                }
+
+                for (size_t iTile = kTile + 1; iTile < tileCount; ++iTile) {
+                    const size_t rowBegin = iTile * kTileSize;
+                    const size_t rowEnd = std::min(n, rowBegin + kTileSize);
+                    const size_t columnToken = iTile * tileCount + kTile;
+
+#pragma omp task firstprivate(rowBegin, rowEnd, kBegin, kEnd, diagonalToken, columnToken) depend(in : tokens[diagonalToken]) depend(inout : tokens[columnToken]) shared(success) priority(1)
+                    {
+                        if (success.load(std::memory_order_relaxed)) {
+                            solveBlockColumn(data, n, rowBegin, rowEnd, kBegin, kEnd);
+                        }
+                    }
+                }
+
+                for (size_t iTile = kTile + 1; iTile < tileCount; ++iTile) {
+                    const size_t rowBegin = iTile * kTileSize;
+                    const size_t rowEnd = std::min(n, rowBegin + kTileSize);
+                    const size_t rowPanelToken = iTile * tileCount + kTile;
+
+                    for (size_t jTile = kTile + 1; jTile <= iTile; ++jTile) {
+                        const size_t colBegin = jTile * kTileSize;
+                        const size_t colEnd = std::min(n, colBegin + kTileSize);
+                        const size_t colPanelToken = jTile * tileCount + kTile;

+

+                            {
+                                if (success.load(std::memory_order_relaxed)) {

+                                                       colBegin, colEnd, kBegin, kEnd);

+                                    updateTrailingTile(data, n, rowBegin, rowEnd,

+                                }
+                            }

+                    }
+                }
+            }

+        }
+    }

+        return false;
+    }

+    // The factorization only consumes the lower triangle. Clear the upper

+    // sections in OpenMP depend clauses.
+    std::vector<unsigned char> dependencyTokens(tileCount * tileCount);
+    unsigned char* const tokens = dependencyTokens.data();
+    std::atomic_bool success{true};
+
+#pragma omp parallel default(none) shared(data, n, tileCount, tokens, success)
+    {
+#pragma omp single
+        {
+            for (size_t kTile = 0; kTile < tileCount; ++kTile) {
+                const size_t kBegin = kTile * kTileSize;
+                const size_t kEnd = std::min(n, kBegin + kTileSize);
+                const size_t diagonalToken = kTile * tileCount + kTile;
+
+#pragma omp task firstprivate(kBegin, kEnd, diagonalToken) depend(inout : tokens[diagonalToken]) shared(success) priority(2)
+                {
+                    if (success.load(std::memory_order_relaxed) &&
+                        !factorDiagonalTile(data, n, kBegin, kEnd)) {
+                        success.store(false, std::memory_order_relaxed);
+                    }
+                }
+
+                for (size_t iTile = kTile + 1; iTile < tileCount; ++iTile) {
+                    const size_t rowBegin = iTile * kTileSize;
+                    const size_t rowEnd = std::min(n, rowBegin + kTileSize);
+                    const size_t columnToken = iTile * tileCount + kTile;
+
+#pragma omp task firstprivate(rowBegin, rowEnd, kBegin, kEnd, diagonalToken, columnToken) depend(in : tokens[diagonalToken]) depend(inout : tokens[columnToken]) shared(success) priority(1)
+                    {
+                        if (success.load(std::memory_order_relaxed)) {
+                            solveBlockColumn(data, n, rowBegin, rowEnd, kBegin, kEnd);
+                        }
+                    }
+                }
+
+                for (size_t iTile = kTile + 1; iTile < tileCount; ++iTile) {
+                    const size_t rowBegin = iTile * kTileSize;
+                    const size_t rowEnd = std::min(n, rowBegin + kTileSize);
+                    const size_t rowPanelToken = iTile * tileCount + kTile;
+
+                    for (size_t jTile = kTile + 1; jTile <= iTile; ++jTile) {
+                        const size_t colBegin = jTile * kTileSize;
+                        const size_t colEnd = std::min(n, colBegin + kTileSize);
+                        const size_t colPanelToken = jTile * tileCount + kTile;
+                        const size_t outputToken = iTile * tileCount + jTile;
+
+                        if (iTile == jTile) {
+#pragma omp task firstprivate(rowBegin, rowEnd, colBegin, colEnd, kBegin, kEnd, rowPanelToken, outputToken) depend(in : tokens[rowPanelToken]) depend(inout : tokens[outputToken]) shared(success) priority(2)
+                            {
+                                if (success.load(std::memory_order_relaxed)) {
+                                    updateTrailingTile(data, n, rowBegin, rowEnd,
+                                                       colBegin, colEnd, kBegin, kEnd);
+                                }
+                            }
+                        } else {

+                            {
+                                if (success.load(std::memory_order_relaxed)) {
+                                    updateTrailingTile(data, n, rowBegin, rowEnd,
+                                                       colBegin, colEnd, kBegin, kEnd);
+                                }
+                            }
+                        }
+                    }
+                }
+            }
+#pragma omp taskwait
+        }
+    }
+
+    if (!success.load(std::memory_order_relaxed)) {
+        return false;
+    }
+
+    // The factorization only consumes the lower triangle. Clear the upper
+    // triangle once, in parallel, after all factorization tasks complete.
+#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
+        std::fill(data + i * n + i + 1, data + (i + 1) * n, 0.0);
+    }
+

+// Cache-blocked, right-looking Cholesky decomposition.  OpenMP task
+// dependencies form the tiled Cholesky DAG, allowing work from later block
+// columns to start as soon as its inputs are ready instead of imposing a
+// global barrier after every phase.
+
+bool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
+    if (n == 0) {
+        return true;
+    }

+    double* const data = A.data();
+    const size_t tileCount = (n + kTileSize - 1) / kTileSize;

+                const size_t diagonalToken = kTile * tileCount + kTile;
+

+                    }
+                }

+                    const size_t columnToken = iTile * tileCount + kTile;
+

+                    {
+                        if (success.load(std::memory_order_relaxed)) {
+                            solveBlockColumn(data, n, rowBegin, rowEnd, kBegin, kEnd);

+// A(i,j) -= L(i,k) * L(j,k)^T. Only the lower half of a diagonal
+// destination tile is stored; off-diagonal destination tiles are rectangular.
+static void updateTrailingTile(double* const A, const size_t n,
+                               const size_t rowBegin, const size_t rowEnd,
+                               const size_t colBegin, const size_t colEnd,
+                               const size_t kBegin, const size_t kEnd) {
+    const bool diagonalTile = rowBegin == colBegin;
+    for (size_t i = rowBegin; i < rowEnd; ++i) {
+        double* const rowI = A + i * n;
+        const size_t lastColumn = diagonalTile ? std::min(colEnd, i + 1) : colEnd;
+        for (size_t j = colBegin; j < lastColumn; ++j) {
+            const double* const rowJ = A + j * n;
+            double sum = 0.0;
+#pragma omp simd reduction(+ : sum)
+            for (size_t p = kBegin; p < kEnd; ++p) {
+                sum += rowI[p] * rowJ[p];
+            }
+            rowI[j] -= sum;
+        }
+    }
+}

+// Cache-blocked, right-looking Cholesky decomposition.  OpenMP task
+// dependencies form the tiled Cholesky DAG, allowing work from later block
+// columns to start as soon as its inputs are ready instead of imposing a
+// global barrier after every phase.
+
+bool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
+    if (n == 0) {
+        return true;
+    }
+
+    double* const data = A.data();
+    const size_t tileCount = (n + kTileSize - 1) / kTileSize;
+
+    // One byte per tile is sufficient: dependencies use addresses as tokens,
+    // not the token values.  It also avoids non-contiguous row-major tile
+    // sections in OpenMP depend clauses.
+    std::vector<unsigned char> dependencyTokens(tileCount * tileCount);
+    unsigned char* const tokens = dependencyTokens.data();
+    std::atomic_bool success{true};
+
+#pragma omp parallel default(none) shared(data, n, tileCount, tokens, success)
+    {
+#pragma omp single
+        {
+            for (size_t kTile = 0; kTile < tileCount; ++kTile) {
+                const size_t kBegin = kTile * kTileSize;
+                const size_t kEnd = std::min(n, kBegin + kTileSize);
+                const size_t diagonalToken = kTile * tileCount + kTile;
+
+#pragma omp task firstprivate(kBegin, kEnd, diagonalToken) depend(inout : tokens[diagonalToken]) shared(success) priority(2)
+                {
+                    if (success.load(std::memory_order_relaxed) &&
+                        !factorDiagonalTile(data, n, kBegin, kEnd)) {
+                        success.store(false, std::memory_order_relaxed);
+                    }
+                }
+
+                for (size_t iTile = kTile + 1; iTile < tileCount; ++iTile) {
+                    const size_t rowBegin = iTile * kTileSize;
+                    const size_t rowEnd = std::min(n, rowBegin + kTileSize);
+                    const size_t columnToken = iTile * tileCount + kTile;
+
+#pragma omp task firstprivate(rowBegin, rowEnd, kBegin, kEnd, diagonalToken, columnToken) depend(in : tokens[diagonalToken]) depend(inout : tokens[columnToken]) shared(success) priority(1)
+                    {
+                        if (success.load(std::memory_order_relaxed)) {
+                            solveBlockColumn(data, n, rowBegin, rowEnd, kBegin, kEnd);
+                        }
+                    }
+                }
+
+                for (size_t iTile = kTile + 1; iTile < tileCount; ++iTile) {
+                    const size_t rowBegin = iTile * kTileSize;
+                    const size_t rowEnd = std::min(n, rowBegin + kTileSize);
+                    const size_t rowPanelToken = iTile * tileCount + kTile;
+
+                    for (size_t jTile = kTile + 1; jTile <= iTile; ++jTile) {
+                        const size_t colBegin = jTile * kTileSize;
+                        const size_t colEnd = std::min(n, colBegin + kTileSize);
+                        const size_t colPanelToken = jTile * tileCount + kTile;
+                        const size_t outputToken = iTile * tileCount + jTile;
+
+                        if (iTile == jTile) {
+#pragma omp task firstprivate(rowBegin, rowEnd, colBegin, colEnd, kBegin, kEnd, rowPanelToken, outputToken) depend(in : tokens[rowPanelToken]) depend(inout : tokens[outputToken]) shared(success) priority(2)
+                            {
+                                if (success.load(std::memory_order_relaxed)) {
+                                    updateTrailingTile(data, n, rowBegin, rowEnd,
+                                                       colBegin, colEnd, kBegin, kEnd);
+                                }
+                            }
+                        } else {
+#pragma omp task firstprivate(rowBegin, rowEnd, colBegin, colEnd, kBegin, kEnd, rowPanelToken, colPanelToken, outputToken) depend(in : tokens[rowPanelToken], tokens[colPanelToken]) depend(inout : tokens[outputToken]) shared(success)
+                            {
+                                if (success.load(std::memory_order_relaxed)) {
+                                    updateTrailingTile(data, n, rowBegin, rowEnd,
+                                                       colBegin, colEnd, kBegin, kEnd);
+                                }
+                            }
+                        }
+                    }
+                }
+            }
+#pragma omp taskwait
+        }
+    }
+
+    if (!success.load(std::memory_order_relaxed)) {
+        return false;
+    }
+
+    // The factorization only consumes the lower triangle. Clear the upper
+    // triangle once, in parallel, after all factorization tasks complete.
+#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
+        std::fill(data + i * n + i + 1, data + (i + 1) * n, 0.0);
+    }
+
     return true;
 }

codex
The first tuning pass shows a 96×96 tile is the best balance here: it retains enough ready tasks for 32–64 cores while improving each core’s cache reuse. The remaining hot spot is the trailing update, so I’m now increasing instruction-level parallelism there by accumulating four output entries per vectorized dot-product pass.

+// Solve A(rowBegin:rowEnd, kBegin:kEnd) against the factored diagonal tile.
+static void solveBlockColumn(double* const A, const size_t n,
+                             const size_t rowBegin, const size_t rowEnd,
+                             const size_t kBegin, const size_t kEnd) {
+    for (size_t i = rowBegin; i < rowEnd; ++i) {
+        double* const rowI = A + i * n;
+        for (size_t j = kBegin; j < kEnd; ++j) {
+            const double* const rowJ = A + j * n;
+            double sum = 0.0;
+#pragma omp simd reduction(+ : sum)
+            for (size_t p = kBegin; p < j; ++p) {
+                sum += rowI[p] * rowJ[p];
+            }
+            rowI[j] = (rowI[j] - sum) / rowJ[j];
+        }
+    }
+}

+// A(i,j) -= L(i,k) * L(j,k)^T. Only the lower half of a diagonal
+// destination tile is stored; off-diagonal destination tiles are rectangular.
+static void updateTrailingTile(double* const A, const size_t n,
+                               const size_t rowBegin, const size_t rowEnd,
+                               const size_t colBegin, const size_t colEnd,
+                               const size_t kBegin, const size_t kEnd) {
+    const bool diagonalTile = rowBegin == colBegin;
+    for (size_t i = rowBegin; i < rowEnd; ++i) {
+        double* const rowI = A + i * n;
+        const size_t lastColumn = diagonalTile ? std::min(colEnd, i + 1) : colEnd;
+        size_t j = colBegin;
+
+        // Four independent accumulators hide vector-add latency and reuse the
+        // row from the block column across four output entries.
+        for (; j + 3 < lastColumn; j += 4) {
+            const double* const rowJ0 = A + j * n;
+            const double* const rowJ1 = rowJ0 + n;
+            const double* const rowJ2 = rowJ1 + n;
+            const double* const rowJ3 = rowJ2 + n;

+            double sum1 = 0.0;
+            double sum2 = 0.0;
+            double sum3 = 0.0;
+#pragma omp simd reduction(+ : sum0, sum1, sum2, sum3)
+            for (size_t p = kBegin; p < kEnd; ++p) {
+                const double value = rowI[p];
+                sum0 += value * rowJ0[p];
+                sum1 += value * rowJ1[p];
+                sum2 += value * rowJ2[p];
+                sum3 += value * rowJ3[p];
+            }
+            rowI[j] -= sum0;
+            rowI[j + 1] -= sum1;
+            rowI[j + 2] -= sum2;
+            rowI[j + 3] -= sum3;
+        }
+
+        for (; j < lastColumn; ++j) {
+            const double* const rowJ = A + j * n;
+            double sum = 0.0;
+#pragma omp simd reduction(+ : sum)
+            for (size_t p = kBegin; p < kEnd; ++p) {
+                sum += rowI[p] * rowJ[p];
+            }
+            rowI[j] -= sum;
+        }
+    }

+// Cache-blocked, right-looking Cholesky decomposition.  OpenMP task
+// dependencies form the tiled Cholesky DAG, allowing work from later block
+// columns to start as soon as its inputs are ready instead of imposing a
+// global barrier after every phase.
+
+bool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
+    if (n == 0) {
+        return true;
+    }
+
+    double* const data = A.data();
+    const size_t tileCount = (n + kTileSize - 1) / kTileSize;
+
+    // One byte per tile is sufficient: dependencies use addresses as tokens,
+    // not the token values.  It also avoids non-contiguous row-major tile
+    // sections in OpenMP depend clauses.
+    std::vector<unsigned char> dependencyTokens(tileCount * tileCount);
+    unsigned char* const tokens = dependencyTokens.data();
+    std::atomic_bool success{true};
+
+#pragma omp parallel default(none) shared(data, n, tileCount, tokens, success)
+    {
+#pragma omp single
+        {
+            for (size_t kTile = 0; kTile < tileCount; ++kTile) {
+                const size_t kBegin = kTile * kTileSize;
+                const size_t kEnd = std::min(n, kBegin + kTileSize);
+                const size_t diagonalToken = kTile * tileCount + kTile;
+
+#pragma omp task firstprivate(kBegin, kEnd, diagonalToken) depend(inout : tokens[diagonalToken]) shared(success) priority(2)

+                    if (success.load(std::memory_order_relaxed) &&
+                        !factorDiagonalTile(data, n, kBegin, kEnd)) {
+                        success.store(false, std::memory_order_relaxed);
+                    }
+                }
+
+                for (size_t iTile = kTile + 1; iTile < tileCount; ++iTile) {
+                    const size_t rowBegin = iTile * kTileSize;
+                    const size_t rowEnd = std::min(n, rowBegin + kTileSize);
+                    const size_t columnToken = iTile * tileCount + kTile;
+
+#pragma omp task firstprivate(rowBegin, rowEnd, kBegin, kEnd, diagonalToken, columnToken) depend(in : tokens[diagonalToken]) depend(inout : tokens[columnToken]) shared(success) priority(1)
+                    {
+                        if (success.load(std::memory_order_relaxed)) {
+                            solveBlockColumn(data, n, rowBegin, rowEnd, kBegin, kEnd);
+                        }
+                    }
+                }
+
+                for (size_t iTile = kTile + 1; iTile < tileCount; ++iTile) {
+                    const size_t rowBegin = iTile * kTileSize;
+                    const size_t rowEnd = std::min(n, rowBegin + kTileSize);
+                    const size_t rowPanelToken = iTile * tileCount + kTile;
+
+                    for (size_t jTile = kTile + 1; jTile <= iTile; ++jTile) {
+                        const size_t colBegin = jTile * kTileSize;

+                        const size_t colPanelToken = jTile * tileCount + kTile;
+                        const size_t outputToken = iTile * tileCount + jTile;

+                            {
+                                if (success.load(std::memory_order_relaxed)) {
+                                    updateTrailingTile(data, n, rowBegin, rowEnd,
+                                                       colBegin, colEnd, kBegin, kEnd);
+                                }
+                            }
+                        } else {
+#pragma omp task firstprivate(rowBegin, rowEnd, colBegin, colEnd, kBegin, kEnd, rowPanelToken, colPanelToken, outputToken) depend(in : tokens[rowPanelToken], tokens[colPanelToken]) depend(inout : tokens[outputToken]) shared(success)

+                                if (success.load(std::memory_order_relaxed)) {
+                                    updateTrailingTile(data, n, rowBegin, rowEnd,
+                                                       colBegin, colEnd, kBegin, kEnd);
+                                }
+                            }
+                        }
+                    }
                 }

+    if (!success.load(std::memory_order_relaxed)) {

+    // triangle once, in parallel, after all factorization tasks complete.

+static void updateTrailingTile(double* const A, const size_t n,

+// Cache-blocked, right-looking Cholesky decomposition.  OpenMP task
+// dependencies form the tiled Cholesky DAG, allowing work from later block
+// columns to start as soon as its inputs are ready instead of imposing a
+// global barrier after every phase.
+
+bool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
+    if (n == 0) {
+        return true;
+    }
+
+    double* const data = A.data();
+    const size_t tileCount = (n + kTileSize - 1) / kTileSize;
+
+    // One byte per tile is sufficient: dependencies use addresses as tokens,
+    // not the token values.  It also avoids non-contiguous row-major tile

+    std::vector<unsigned char> dependencyTokens(tileCount * tileCount);
+    unsigned char* const tokens = dependencyTokens.data();
+    std::atomic_bool success{true};
+

+    {
+#pragma omp single

+            for (size_t kTile = 0; kTile < tileCount; ++kTile) {

+                const size_t kEnd = std::min(n, kBegin + kTileSize);
+                const size_t diagonalToken = kTile * tileCount + kTile;
+
+#pragma omp task firstprivate(kBegin, kEnd, diagonalToken) depend(inout : tokens[diagonalToken]) shared(success) priority(2)

+                    const size_t columnToken = iTile * tileCount + kTile;
+
+#pragma omp task firstprivate(rowBegin, rowEnd, kBegin, kEnd, diagonalToken, columnToken) depend(in : tokens[diagonalToken]) depend(inout : tokens[columnToken]) shared(success) priority(1)
+                    {
+                        if (success.load(std::memory_order_relaxed)) {
+                            solveBlockColumn(data, n, rowBegin, rowEnd, kBegin, kEnd);
+                        }
+                    }
+                }
+
+                for (size_t iTile = kTile + 1; iTile < tileCount; ++iTile) {
+                    const size_t rowBegin = iTile * kTileSize;
+                    const size_t rowEnd = std::min(n, rowBegin + kTileSize);
+                    const size_t rowPanelToken = iTile * tileCount + kTile;
+
+                    for (size_t jTile = kTile + 1; jTile <= iTile; ++jTile) {
+                        const size_t colBegin = jTile * kTileSize;
+                        const size_t colEnd = std::min(n, colBegin + kTileSize);
+                        const size_t colPanelToken = jTile * tileCount + kTile;
+                        const size_t outputToken = iTile * tileCount + jTile;
+

+                            {
+                                if (success.load(std::memory_order_relaxed)) {
+                                    updateTrailingTile(data, n, rowBegin, rowEnd,
+                                                       colBegin, colEnd, kBegin, kEnd);
+                                }
+                            }

+                                    updateTrailingTile(data, n, rowBegin, rowEnd,
+                                                       colBegin, colEnd, kBegin, kEnd);
+                                }
+                            }
+                        }
+                    }
                 }
-                A[i * n + j] = (A[i * n + j] - sum) / A[j * n + j];

+
+    if (!success.load(std::memory_order_relaxed)) {
+        return false;

+    // The factorization only consumes the lower triangle. Clear the upper
+    // triangle once, in parallel, after all factorization tasks complete.
+#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
+        std::fill(data + i * n + i + 1, data + (i + 1) * n, 0.0);

+// Solve A(rowBegin:rowEnd, kBegin:kEnd) against the factored diagonal tile.
+static void solveBlockColumn(double* const A, const size_t n,
+                             const size_t rowBegin, const size_t rowEnd,
+                             const size_t kBegin, const size_t kEnd) {
+    for (size_t i = rowBegin; i < rowEnd; ++i) {
+        double* const rowI = A + i * n;
+        for (size_t j = kBegin; j < kEnd; ++j) {
+            const double* const rowJ = A + j * n;
+            double sum = 0.0;
+#pragma omp simd reduction(+ : sum)
+            for (size_t p = kBegin; p < j; ++p) {
+                sum += rowI[p] * rowJ[p];
+            }
+            rowI[j] = (rowI[j] - sum) / rowJ[j];
+        }
+    }

+// A(i,j) -= L(i,k) * L(j,k)^T. Only the lower half of a diagonal
+// destination tile is stored; off-diagonal destination tiles are rectangular.
+static void updateTrailingTile(double* const A, const size_t n,
+                               const size_t rowBegin, const size_t rowEnd,
+                               const size_t colBegin, const size_t colEnd,
+                               const size_t kBegin, const size_t kEnd) {
+    const bool diagonalTile = rowBegin == colBegin;
+    for (size_t i = rowBegin; i < rowEnd; ++i) {
+        double* const rowI = A + i * n;
+        const size_t lastColumn = diagonalTile ? std::min(colEnd, i + 1) : colEnd;
+        size_t j = colBegin;
+
+        // Independent accumulators hide vector-add latency and reuse the row
+        // from the block column across eight output entries.
+        for (; j + 7 < lastColumn; j += 8) {

+            const double* const rowJ1 = rowJ0 + n;

+            double sum4 = 0.0;
+            double sum5 = 0.0;
+            double sum6 = 0.0;
+            double sum7 = 0.0;
+#pragma omp simd reduction(+ : sum0, sum1, sum2, sum3, sum4, sum5, sum6, sum7)
+            for (size_t p = kBegin; p < kEnd; ++p) {
+                const double value = rowI[p];
+                sum0 += value * rowJ0[p];
+                sum1 += value * rowJ1[p];
+                sum2 += value * rowJ2[p];
+                sum3 += value * rowJ3[p];
+                sum4 += value * rowJ4[p];
+                sum5 += value * rowJ5[p];
+                sum6 += value * rowJ6[p];
+                sum7 += value * rowJ7[p];
+            }
+            rowI[j] -= sum0;
+            rowI[j + 1] -= sum1;
+            rowI[j + 2] -= sum2;
+            rowI[j + 3] -= sum3;
+            rowI[j + 4] -= sum4;
+            rowI[j + 5] -= sum5;
+            rowI[j + 6] -= sum6;
+            rowI[j + 7] -= sum7;

+        for (; j < lastColumn; ++j) {
+            const double* const rowJ = A + j * n;
+            double sum = 0.0;
+#pragma omp simd reduction(+ : sum)
+            for (size_t p = kBegin; p < kEnd; ++p) {
+                sum += rowI[p] * rowJ[p];
+            }
+            rowI[j] -= sum;
+        }
+    }

+// Cache-blocked, right-looking Cholesky decomposition.  OpenMP task
+// dependencies form the tiled Cholesky DAG, allowing work from later block
+// columns to start as soon as its inputs are ready instead of imposing a
+// global barrier after every phase.

+bool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
+    if (n == 0) {
+        return true;
+    }
+
+    double* const data = A.data();
+    const size_t tileCount = (n + kTileSize - 1) / kTileSize;
+

+    // not the token values.  It also avoids non-contiguous row-major tile

+    std::vector<unsigned char> dependencyTokens(tileCount * tileCount);
+    unsigned char* const tokens = dependencyTokens.data();

+

+                    {
+                        if (success.load(std::memory_order_relaxed)) {
+                            solveBlockColumn(data, n, rowBegin, rowEnd, kBegin, kEnd);
+                        }
+                    }

+                    const size_t rowBegin = iTile * kTileSize;

+                    const size_t rowPanelToken = iTile * tileCount + kTile;
